--- [STEP 74] Create Autolist Evergreen Queue ---
>> REQUEST:
curl -X POST 'http://localhost:5000/v1/autolists?brandId=6abdee125311b5c505f91ce2' \
  -H 'Authorization: Bearer eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiIsInR5cCI6IkpXVCJ9.eyJleHAiOjE3OTA4MzMwNDYsImlhdCI6MTc5MDgzMjE0Niwic3ViIjoiNmFiZGU2NGI4ODBjNjRjMWRiOTdhZWEzIn0.5hEY1Q08cPnl-auCXZftqSyCrrCYFdC_qKZTMc5bbkI' \
